          <div data-target="custom-scopes.customScopesModalDialogFlash"></div>

          <input type="hidden" id="custom_scope_id" name="custom_scope_id" data-target="custom-scopes.customScopesIdField">

          <div class="form-group">
            <label for="custom_scope_name">Name</label>
            <auto-check src="/search/custom_scopes/check_name" required>
              <input
                type="text"
                name="custom_scope_name"
                id="custom_scope_name"
                data-target="custom-scopes.customScopesNameField"
                class="form-control"
                autocomplete="off"
                placeholder="github-ruby"
                required
                maxlength="50">
              <input type="hidden" data-csrf="true" value="hY0HtH4qJZwYB0WAKuF38rvTgXohOtrHChIwAoukkqSlY3oDagZxJqflHkv3PyMAr7onOuFa06gsl8uHMct+cQ==" />
            </auto-check>
          </div>

          <div class="form-group">
            <label for="custom_scope_query">Query</label>
            <input
              type="text"
              name="custom_scope_query"
              id="custom_scope_query"
              data-target="custom-scopes.customScopesQueryField"
              class="form-control"
              autocomplete="off"
              placeholder="(repo:mona/a OR repo:mona/b) AND lang:python"
              required
              maxlength="500">
          </div>

          <p class="text-small color-fg-muted">
            To see all available qualifiers, see our <a class="Link--inTextBlock" href="https://docs.github.com/search-github/github-code-search/understanding-github-code-search-syntax">documentation</a>.

</button>
</div>

dir=\"auto\"\u003e\u003ch2 tabindex=\"-1\" class=\"heading-element\" dir=\"auto\"\u003eFeatures\u003c/h2\u003e\u003ca id=\"user-content-features\" class=\"anchor\" aria-label=\"Permalink: Features\" 2.83Z\"\u003e\u003c/path\u003e\u003c/svg\u003e\u003c/a\u003e\u003c/div\u003e\n\u003cul dir=\"auto\"\u003e\n\u003cli\u003e🗂️ Parsing of \u003ca href=\"https://docling-project.github.io/docling/usage/supported_formats/\" rel=\"nofollow\"\u003emultiple document formats\u003c/a\u003e incl. PDF, DOCX, PPTX, XLSX, HTML, WAV, MP3, WebVTT, images (PNG, TIFF, JPEG, ...), LaTeX, plain text, and more\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003e📑 Advanced PDF understanding incl. page layout, reading order, table structure, code, formulas, image classification, and more\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003e🧬 Unified, expressive \u003ca href=\"https://docling-project.github.io/docling/concepts/docling_document/\" rel=\"nofollow\"\u003eDoclingDocument\u003c/a\u003e representation format\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003e↪️ Various \u003ca href=\"https://docling-project.github.io/docling/usage/supported_formats/\" rel=\"nofollow\"\u003eexport formats\u003c/a\u003e and options, including Markdown, HTML, WebVTT, \u003ca href=\"https://arxiv.org/abs/2503.11576\" rel=\"nofollow\"\u003eDocTags\u003c/a\u003e and lossless JSON\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003e📜 Support of several application-specifc XML schemas incl. \u003ca href=\"https://www.uspto.gov/patents\" rel=\"nofollow\"\u003eUSPTO\u003c/a\u003e patents, \u003ca href=\"https://jats.nlm.nih.gov/\" rel=\"nofollow\"\u003eJATS\u003c/a\u003e articles, and \u003ca href=\"https://www.xbrl.org/\" rel=\"nofollow\"\u003eXBRL\u003c/a\u003e financial reports.\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003e🔒 Local execution capabilities for sensitive data and air-gapped environments\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003e🤖 Plug-and-play \u003ca href=\"https://docling-project.github.io/docling/integrations/\" rel=\"nofollow\"\u003eintegrations\u003c/a\u003e incl. LangChain, LlamaIndex, Crew AI \u0026amp; Haystack for agentic AI\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003e🔍 Extensive OCR support for scanned PDFs and images\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003e👓 Support of several Visual Language Models (\u003ca href=\"https://huggingface.co/ibm-granite/granite-docling-258M\" rel=\"nofollow\"\u003eGraniteDocling\u003c/a\u003e)\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003e🎙️ Audio support with Automatic Speech Recognition (ASR) models\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003e🔌 Connect to any agent using the \u003ca href=\"https://docling-project.github.io/docling/usage/mcp/\" rel=\"nofollow\"\u003eMCP server\u003c/a\u003e\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003e💻 Simple and convenient CLI\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003c/ul\u003e\n\u003cdiv class=\"markdown-heading\" dir=\"auto\"\u003e\u003ch3 tabindex=\"-1\" dir=\"auto\"\u003e\n\u003cli\u003e📤 Structured \u003ca href=\"https://docling-project.github.io/docling/examples/extraction/\" rel=\"nofollow\"\u003einformation extraction\u003c/a\u003e [🧪 beta]\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003e📑 New layout model (\u003cstrong\u003eHeron\u003c/strong\u003e) by default, for faster PDF parsing\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003e🔌 \u003ca href=\"https://docling-project.github.io/docling/usage/mcp/\" rel=\"nofollow\"\u003eMCP server\u003c/a\u003e for agentic applications\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003e💼 Parsing of XBRL (eXtensible Business Reporting Language) documents for financial reports\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003e💬 Parsing of WebVTT (Web Video Text Tracks) files and export to WebVTT format\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003e💬 Parsing of LaTeX files\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003e📝 Parsing of plain-text files (\u003ccode\u003e.txt\u003c/code\u003e, \u003ccode\u003e.text\u003c/code\u003e) and Markdown supersets (\u003ccode\u003e.qmd\u003c/code\u003e, \u003ccode\u003e.Rmd\u003c/code\u003e)\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003e📝 Chart understanding (Barchart, Piechart, LinePlot): converting them into tables, code or adding detailed descriptions\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003c/ul\u003e\n\u003cdiv class=\"markdown-heading\" dir=\"auto\"\u003e\u003ch3 tabindex=\"-1\" class=\"heading-element\" dir=\"auto\"\u003eComing <div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h2 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">What is Docling ?</h2><a id="user-content-what-is-docling-" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: What is Docling ?" href="#what-is-docling-"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<p dir="auto">Docling simplifies document processing, parsing diverse formats — including advanced PDF understanding — and providing seamless integrations with the gen AI ecosystem.</p>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h2 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">Features</h2><a id="user-content-features" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Features" href="#features"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<ul dir="auto">
<li>🗂️ Parsing of <a href="https://docling-project.github.io/docling/usage/supported_formats/" rel="nofollow">multiple document formats</a> incl. PDF, DOCX, PPTX, XLSX, HTML, WAV, MP3, WebVTT, images (PNG, TIFF, JPEG, ...), LaTeX, plain text, and more</li>
<li>📑 Advanced PDF understanding incl. page layout, reading order, table structure, code, formulas, image classification, and more</li>
<li>🧬 Unified, expressive <a href="https://docling-project.github.io/docling/concepts/docling_document/" rel="nofollow">DoclingDocument</a> representation format</li>
<li>↪️ Various <a href="https://docling-project.github.io/docling/usage/supported_formats/" rel="nofollow">export formats</a> and options, including Markdown, HTML, WebVTT, <a href="https://arxiv.org/abs/2503.11576" rel="nofollow">DocTags</a> and lossless JSON</li>
<li>📜 Support of several application-specifc XML schemas incl. <a href="https://www.uspto.gov/patents" rel="nofollow">USPTO</a> patents, <a href="https://jats.nlm.nih.gov/" rel="nofollow">JATS</a> articles, and <a href="https://www.xbrl.org/" rel="nofollow">XBRL</a> financial reports.</li>
<li>🔒 Local execution capabilities for sensitive data and air-gapped environments</li>
<li>🤖 Plug-and-play <a href="https://docling-project.github.io/docling/integrations/" rel="nofollow">integrations</a> incl. LangChain, LlamaIndex, Crew AI &amp; Haystack for agentic AI</li>
<li>🔍 Extensive OCR support for scanned PDFs and images</li>
<li>👓 Support of several Visual Language Models (<a href="https://huggingface.co/ibm-granite/granite-docling-258M" rel="nofollow">GraniteDocling</a>)</li>
<li>🎙️ Audio support with Automatic Speech Recognition (ASR) models</li>
<li>🔌 Connect to any agent using the <a href="https://docling-project.github.io/docling/usage/mcp/" rel="nofollow">MCP server</a></li>
<li>💻 Simple and convenient CLI</li>
</ul>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h3 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">What's new</h3><a id="user-content-whats-new" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: What's new" href="#whats-new"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<ul dir="auto">
<li>📤 Structured <a href="https://docling-project.github.io/docling/examples/extraction/" rel="nofollow">information extraction</a> [🧪 beta]</li>
<li>📑 New layout model (<strong>Heron</strong>) by default, for faster PDF parsing</li>
<li>🔌 <a href="https://docling-project.github.io/docling/usage/mcp/" rel="nofollow">MCP server</a> for agentic applications</li>
<li>💼 Parsing of XBRL (eXtensible Business Reporting Language) documents for financial reports</li>
<li>💬 Parsing of WebVTT (Web Video Text Tracks) files and export to WebVTT format</li>
<li>💬 Parsing of LaTeX files</li>
<li>📝 Parsing of plain-text files (<code>.txt</code>, <code>.text</code>) and Markdown supersets (<code>.qmd</code>, <code>.Rmd</code>)</li>
<li>📝 Chart understanding (Barchart, Piechart, LinePlot): converting them into tables, code or adding detailed descriptions</li>
</ul>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h3 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">Coming soon</h3><a id="user-content-coming-soon" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Coming soon" href="#coming-soon"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<ul dir="auto">
<li>📝 Metadata extraction, including title, authors, references &amp; language</li>
<li>📝 Complex chemistry understanding (Molecular structures)</li>
</ul>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h2 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">Quickstart</h2><a id="user-content-quickstart" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Quickstart" href="#quickstart"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h3 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">1. Install</h3><a id="user-content-1-install" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: 1. Install" href="#1-install"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-shell notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" dir="auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="pip install docling"><pre>pip install docling</pre></div>
<blockquote>
<p dir="auto"><strong>Note:</strong> Python 3.9 support was dropped in docling version 2.70.0. Please use Python 3.10 or higher.</p>
</blockquote>
<p dir="auto">Works on macOS, Linux and Windows environments. Both x86_64 and arm64 architectures.</p>
<p dir="auto">More <a href="https://docling-project.github.io/docling/installation/" rel="nofollow">detailed installation instructions</a> are available in the docs.</p>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h2 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">2. Convert a document (CLI)</h2><a id="user-content-2-convert-a-document-cli" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: 2. Convert a document (CLI)" href="#2-convert-a-document-cli"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-shell notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" dir="auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="docling https://arxiv.org/pdf/2206.01062"><pre>docling https://arxiv.org/pdf/2206.01062</pre></div>
<p dir="auto">This generates a .md file in the current directory containing structured document content.</p>
<p dir="auto">You can also use 🥚<a href="https://huggingface.co/ibm-granite/granite-docling-258M" rel="nofollow">GraniteDocling</a> and other VLMs via Docling CLI:</p>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-shell notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" dir="auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="docling --pipeline vlm --vlm-model granite_docling https://arxiv.org/pdf/2206.01062"><pre>docling --pipeline vlm --vlm-model granite_docling https://arxiv.org/pdf/2206.01062</pre></div>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h2 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">3. Python usage (recommended)</h2><a id="user-content-3-python-usage-recommended" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: 3. Python usage (recommended)" href="#3-python-usage-recommended"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-python notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" dir="auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="from docling.document_converter import DocumentConverter

source = &quot;https://arxiv.org/pdf/2408.09869&quot;  # document per local path or URL
converter = DocumentConverter()
result = converter.convert(source)
print(result.document.export_to_markdown())  # output: &quot;## Docling Technical Report[...]&quot;"><pre><span class="pl-k">from</span> <span class="pl-s1">docling</span>.<span class="pl-s1">document_converter</span> <span class="pl-k">import</span> <span class="pl-v">DocumentConverter</span>

<span class="pl-s1">source</span> <span class="pl-c1">=</span> <span class="pl-s">"https://arxiv.org/pdf/2408.09869"</span>  <span class="pl-c"># document per local path or URL</span>
<span class="pl-s1">converter</span> <span class="pl-c1">=</span> <span class="pl-en">DocumentConverter</span>()
<span class="pl-s1">result</span> <span class="pl-c1">=</span> <span class="pl-s1">converter</span>.<span class="pl-c1">convert</span>(<span class="pl-s1">source</span>)
<span class="pl-en">print</span>(<span class="pl-s1">result</span>.<span class="pl-c1">document</span>.<span class="pl-c1">export_to_markdown</span>())  <span class="pl-c"># output: "## Docling Technical Report[...]"</span></pre></div>
<p dir="auto">More advanced <a href="https://docling-project.github.io/docling/usage/" rel="nofollow">usage</a> and <a href="https://docling-project.github.io/docling/installation/" rel="nofollow">configuration</a> options.</p>
